what is the cost of dx ultrasound-heart?
----------
PREDS:
select sum(cost.cost) from cost where cost.hadm_id in ( select procedures_icd.hadm_id from procedures_icd where procedures_icd.icd9_code = ( select d_icd_procedures.icd9_code from d_icd_procedures where d_icd_procedures.short_title = 'dx ultrasound-heart' ) )
Flattened: {'sel': 'sum', 'agg': ['cost.cost', 'd_icd_procedures.icd9_code', 'procedures_icd.hadm_id'], 'tab': ['cost', 'd_icd_procedures', 'procedures_icd'], 'cond': [['d_icd_procedures.short_title', '=', 'dx ultrasound-heart']]}
----------
TRUTH:
select distinct cost.cost from cost where cost.event_type = 'procedures_icd' and cost.event_id in ( select procedures_icd.row_id from procedures_icd where procedures_icd.icd9_code = ( select d_icd_procedures.icd9_code from d_icd_procedures where d_icd_procedures.short_title = 'dx ultrasound-heart' ) )
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['cost.cost', 'd_icd_procedures.icd9_code', 'procedures_icd.row_id'], 'tab': ['cost', 'd_icd_procedures', 'procedures_icd'], 'cond': [['cost.event_type', '=', 'procedures_icd'], ['d_icd_procedures.short_title', '=', 'dx ultrasound-heart']]}
----------

----------
PREDS:

Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.age', 'admissions.hadm_id', 'd_labitems.itemid', 'labevents.valuenum'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_labitems', 'labevents'], 'cond': [['admissions.dischtime', 'is', 'null'], ['admissions.subject_id', '=', '97395'], ['d_labitems.label', '=', 'hematocrit'], ['t1.valuenum', '=', '37.0']]}
----------
TRUTH:
select distinct t1.c1 from ( select labevents.valuenum, percent_rank() over ( order by labevents.valuenum ) as c1 from labevents where labevents.itemid in ( select d_labitems.itemid from d_labitems where d_labitems.label = 'hematocrit, calculated' ) and labevents.hadm_id in ( select admissions.hadm_id from admissions where admissions.age = ( select admissions.age from admissions where admissions.subject_id = 97395 and admissions.dischtime is null ) ) ) as t1 where t1.valuenum = 37.0
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.age', 'admissions.hadm_id', 'd_labitems.itemid', 'labevents.valuenum'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_labitems', 'labevents'], 'cond': [['admissions.dischtime', 'is', 'null'], ['admissions.subject_id', '=', '97395'], ['d_labitems.label', '=', 'hematocrit, calculated'], ['t1.valuenum', '=', '37.0']]}

what is the one year survival rate of the ch dvt/embl low ext nos patients?
----------
PREDS:
select sum( case when patients.dod is null then 1 when strftime('%j',patients.dod) - strftime('%j',t2.charttime) > 365 then 1 else 0 end ) * 100 / count(*) from ( select t1.subject_id, t1.charttime from ( select admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime from diagnoses_icd join admissions on diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id where diagnoses_icd.icd9_code = ( select d_icd_diagnoses.icd9_code from d_icd_diagnoses where d_icd_diagnoses.short_title = 'ch dvt/embl low ext nos' ) group by admissions.subject_id having min(diagnoses_icd.charttime) = diagnoses_icd.charttime ) as t1 where strftime('%j',current_time) - strftime('%j',t1.charttime) > 365 ) as t2 join patients on t2.subject_id = patients.subject_id
Flattened: {'sel': 'sum', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd9_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'patients.dod', 't1.charttime', 't1.subject_id', 't2.charttime'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'patients'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.short_title', '=', 'ch dvt/embl low ext nos'], ["strftime('%j','2105-12-31 23:59:00') - strftime('%j',t1.charttime)", '>', '365']]}
----------
TRUTH:
select sum( case when patients.dod is null then 1 when strftime('%j',patients.dod) - strftime('%j',t2.charttime) > 1 * 365 then 1 else 0 end ) * 100 / count(*) from ( select t1.subject_id, t1.charttime from ( select admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime from diagnoses_icd join admissions on diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id where diagnoses_icd.icd9_code = ( select d_icd_diagnoses.icd9_code from d_icd_diagnoses where d_icd_diagnoses.short_title = 'ch dvt/embl low ext nos' ) group by admissions.subject_id having min(diagnoses_icd.charttime) = diagnoses_icd.charttime ) as t1 where strftime('%j',current_time) - strftime('%j',t1.charttime) > 1 * 365 ) as t2 join patients on t2.subject_id = patients.subject_id
Flattened: {'sel': 'sum', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd9_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'patients.dod', 't1.charttime', 't1.subject_id', 't2.charttime'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'patients'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.short_title', '=', 'ch dvt/embl low ext nos'], ["strftime('%j','2105-12-31 23:59:00') - strftime('%j',t1.charttime)", '>', '1 * 365']]}
----------

how many patients received a pericardiocentesis procedure within the same month after receiving a open liver biopsy procedure during this year?
----------
PREDS:
select count(distinct t1.subject_id) from (select admissions.subject_id, procedures_icd.charttime from procedures_icd join admissions on procedures_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id where procedures_icd.icd9_code = (select d_icd_procedures.icd9_code from d_icd_procedures where d_icd_procedures.short_title = 'open liver biopsy') and strftime('%Y', procedures_icd.charttime) = strftime('%Y', 'now')) as t1 join (select admissions.subject_id, procedures_icd.charttime from procedures_icd join admissions on procedures_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id where procedures_icd.icd9_code = (select d_icd_procedures.icd9_code from d_icd_procedures where d_icd_procedures.short_title = 'pericardiocentesis') and strftime('%Y', procedures_icd.charttime) = strftime('%Y', 'now')) as t2 on t1.subject_id = t2.subject_id where t1.charttime < t2.charttime and datetime(t1.charttime, 'start of month') = datetime(t2.charttime, 'start of month')
Flattened: {'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_procedures.icd9_code', 'procedures_icd.charttime', 't1.subject_id'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_procedures', 'procedures_icd'], 'cond': [['d_icd_procedures.short_title', '=', 'open liver biopsy'], ['d_icd_procedures.short_title', '=', 'pericardiocentesis'], ["datetime(t1.charttime,'start of month')", '=', "datetime(t2.charttime, 'start of month')"], ["strftime('%y',procedures_icd.charttime)", '=', "strftime('%y', '2105-12-31 23:59:00')"], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.charttime']]}
----------
TRUTH:
select count( distinct t1.subject_id ) from ( select admissions.subject_id, procedures_icd.charttime from procedures_icd join admissions on procedures_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id where procedures_icd.icd9_code = ( select d_icd_procedures.icd9_code from d_icd_procedures where d_icd_procedures.short_title = 'open liver biopsy' ) and datetime(procedures_icd.charttime,'start of year') = datetime(current_time,'start of year','-0 year') ) as t1 join ( select admissions.subject_id, procedures_icd.charttime from procedures_icd join admissions on procedures_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id where procedures_icd.icd9_code = ( select d_icd_procedures.icd9_code from d_icd_procedures where d_icd_procedures.short_title = 'pericardiocentesis' ) and datetime(procedures_icd.charttime,'start of year') = datetime(current_time,'start of year','-0 year') ) as t2 on t1.subject_id = t2.subject_id where t1.charttime < t2.charttime and datetime(t1.charttime,'start of month') = datetime(t2.charttime,'start of month')
Flattened: {'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_procedures.icd9_code', 'procedures_icd.charttime', 't1.subject_id'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_procedures', 'procedures_icd'], 'cond': [['d_icd_procedures.short_title', '=', 'open liver biopsy'], ['d_icd_procedures.short_title', '=', 'pericardiocentesis'], ["datetime(procedures_icd.charttime,'start of year')", '=', "datetime('2105-12-31 23:59:00','start of year','-0 year')"], ["datetime(t1.charttime,'start of month')", '=', "datetime(t2.charttime,'start of month')"], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.charttime']]}

since 4 years ago, how many patients received alcohol detoxification within the same hospital visit after the previous procedure of cont inv mec ven <96 hrs?
----------
PREDS:
select count( distinct t1.subject_id ) from ( select admissions.subject_id, procedures_icd.charttime from procedures_icd join admissions on procedures_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id where procedures_icd.icd9_code = ( select d_icd_procedures.icd9_code from d_icd_procedures where d_icd_procedures.short_title = 'cont inv mec ven <96 hrs' ) and datetime(procedures_icd.charttime) >= datetime(current_time,'-4 year') ) as t1 join ( select admissions.subject_id, procedures_icd.charttime from procedures_icd join admissions on procedures_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id where procedures_icd.icd9_code = ( select d_icd_procedures.icd9_code from d_icd_procedures where d_icd_procedures.short_title = 'alcohol detoxification' ) and datetime(procedures_icd.charttime) >= datetime(current_time,'-4 year') ) as t2 on t1.subject_id = t2.subject_id where t1.charttime < t2.charttime and t1.hadm_id = t2.hadm_id
Flattened: {'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_procedures.icd9_code', 'procedures_icd.charttime', 't1.subject_id'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_procedures', 'procedures_icd'], 'cond': [['d_icd_procedures.short_title', '=', 'alcohol detoxification'], ['d_icd_procedures.short_title', '=', 'cont inv mec ven <96 hrs'], ['datetime(procedures_icd.charttime)', '>=', "datetime('2105-12-31 23:59:00','-4 year')"], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.charttime'], ['t1.hadm_id', '=', 't2.hadm_id']]}
----------

give me the top four most frequent lab tests that patients were given during the same month after receiving percu nephrostm w/o frag since 2105.
----------

what was the five year survival rate for patients who were prescribed chlorhexidine gluconate 0.12% oral rinse after having been diagnosed with upper extremity embolism?
PRED: none
GOLD: select sum( case when patients.dod is null then 1 when strftime('%J',patients.dod) - strftime('%J',t4.charttime) > 5 * 365 then 1 else 0 end ) * 100 / count(*) from ( select t2.subject_id, t2.charttime from ( select t1.subject_id, t1.charttime from ( select admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime from diagnoses_icd join admissions on diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id where diagnoses_icd.icd9_code = ( select d_icd_diagnoses.icd9_code from d_icd_diagnoses where d_icd_diagnoses.short_title = 'upper extremity embolism' ) group by admissions.subject_id having min(diagnoses_icd.charttime) = diagnoses_icd.charttime ) as t1 where strftime('%J','2105-12-31 23:59:00') - strftime('%J',t1.charttime) > 5 * 365 ) as t2 join ( select admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.startdate from prescriptions join admissions on prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id where prescriptions.drug = 'chlorhexidine gluconate 0.12% oral rinse' ) as t3 on t2.subject_id = t3.subject_id where t2.charttime < t3.startdate ) as t4 join patients on t4.subject_id = patients.subject_id

GOLD: select count( distinct t1.subject_id ) from ( select admissions.subject_id, procedures_icd.charttime, admissions.hadm_id from procedures_icd join admissions on procedures_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id where procedures_icd.icd9_code = ( select d_icd_procedures.icd9_code from d_icd_procedures where d_icd_procedures.short_title = 'contrast aortogram' ) and strftime('%Y',procedures_icd.charttime) = '2102' ) as t1 join ( select admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.startdate, admissions.hadm_id from prescriptions join admissions on prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id where prescriptions.drug = 'furosemide' and strftime('%Y',prescriptions.startdate) = '2102' ) as t2 on t1.subject_id = t2.subject_id where t1.charttime < t2.startdate and t1.hadm_id = t2.hadm_id

count the number of patients who had been prescribed tacrolimus within the same month after a percu abdominal drainage procedure until 4 years ago.
PRED: select count(distinct t1.subject_id) from (select admissions.subject_id, procedures_icd.charttime, admissions.hadm_id from procedures_icd join admissions on procedures_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id where procedures_icd.icd9_code = (select d_icd_procedures.icd9_code from d_icd_procedures where d_icd_procedures.short_title = 'percu abdominal drainage') and procedures_icd.charttime >= datetime('2105-12-31 23:59:00', '-4 years')) as t1 join (select admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.startdate, admissions.hadm_id from prescriptions join admissions on prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id where prescriptions.drug = 'tacrolimus') as t2 on t1.subject_id = t2.subject_id where strftime('%m', t1.charttime) = strftime('%m', t2.startdate) and t1.hadm_id = t2.hadm_id
GOLD: select count( distinct t1.subject_id ) from ( select admissions.subject_id, procedures_icd.charttime from procedures_icd join admissions on procedures_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id where procedures_icd.icd9_code = ( select d_icd_procedures.icd9_code from d_icd_procedures where d_icd_procedures.short_title = 'percu abdominal drainage' ) and datetime(procedures_icd.charttime) <= datetime('2105-12-31 23:59:00','-4 year') ) as t1 join ( select admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.startdate from prescriptions join admissions on prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id where prescriptions.drug = 'tacrolimus' and datetime(prescriptions.startdate) <= datetime('2105-12-31 23:59:00','-4 year') ) as t2 on t1.subject_id = t2.subject_id where t1.charttime < t2.startdate and datetime(t1.charttime,'start of month') = datetime(t2.startdate,'start of month')
